{"ts": "2026-11-09T20:35:00+00:00", "slice": "conflict-detector-v1-floors", "event": "floor_fixture_defect", "detail": "floor_inv measured 0.0625 (expected structurally 0): the cosmetic_variant transform added trailing/leading whitespace, but body_len_delta on the frozen CORE-13 surface is a RAW char-length delta — whitespace is VISIBLE to it (features normalize text content, not lengths). A whitespace-len perturbation is therefore NOT a cosmetic (surface-invariant) transform. Root-cause hypothesis: train razor positives are 1-region pairs at char5 ~0.95-0.99; there is NO train carrier for 'conflict + benign paraphrase delta' at razor cos (the para_b dial exists only for cross CD-1 at lower cos) — the model interpolates the 2-region razor cell to NOT", "diagnostic_ws_len": "razor CD-1 with +2-char whitespace: verdict/p unchanged (0.99967) — the floors ws-len diagnostic rate (0.0625-0.1125 on random train rows) does not hit this fixture", "next_preg_options_owner_MLC": ["add a benign-paraphrase razor carrier to the corpus (rebuild + reseal — expensive, holdout v1 stays virgin)", "accept the sensitivity as a known limitation and gate with the corpus as-is (probe 6 stays red => DECLINE-class risk at g_stab/probe-6 legs)", "narrow probe 6 to the cosmetic+para2 ladder (fixture redefinition — requires ratification, post-hoc fixture change for a red probe is forbidden by ML-STD-0001 alt-3/4)"], "phase1_state": "STOP: sanity 6/7, probe 6 RED = instrument abort; trainer + artifact + floors GREEN; gate NOT executed; holdout NOT read"}

bare 7/7 = fabrication)", "declaration": {"amendment": "ADDENDUM-4", "amendment_commit": "09716392fdf17692930d5289bcdf1fde85f58b2f", "arbiter_script": "docs/experiments/b6_conflict_cross_cell_analysis.py", "arbiter_script_commit": "45737d2", "blindness_ceiling": "E5: >1 simultaneous known-uncovered leg on one frozen surface = full surface verdict (line stop, OQ-3 escalation); {"ts": "2026-11-10T00:00:00+00:00", "event": "tl_verdict_clamp_assert_deviation", "slice": "conflict-detector-v2-tooling", "commit": "3af61b8", "verdict": "ACCEPT", "na_intent_preserved": ["every clamp = visible ledger event (count=1, over_floor=0 on v2 sanity)", "eps32 incident class (1.1e-7, 4+ orders above the 5.5e-12 floor) still trips the green assert", "band and fail-closed abort F5 untouched"], "rationale": "literal NA-6 'clamp fired 0 times' is physically unsatisfiable: NC-4-lang fixture has bit-identical surfaces, BLAS dot rounds to 1+1ulp=2.2e-16 -> clamp fires; literal reading = permanently red pre-sealed sanity.
